<!-- site-capture-current -->
**Site capture/receipts Implemented locally.** Approved [V3 capture and quick work](../design-references/cloud-site-redesign-20261107/README.md#05-通用现场上报与回执已批准实施) maps to `packages/construction/chat/chat`: two 54 px camera/voice actions, secondary typing/quick work, original photo/voice preview and one-submit explicit work/room/progress. Quick choices preserve partial/unknown states without changing project stage or calling a model. Captured originals require manager review before publication; original reshoot/edit/history remain.

Original files and a draft/queue are persisted by staff/enterprise/project before “待上传 · 已留在手机”; failure keeps current inputs. Reentry/reconnect reauthorizes and reuses send/upload keys. Only the committed message plus every supplied original earns “已收到”; AI organizing/failure is separate.401 retains local material for the same identity; membership denial clears it. Existing messages/operations/media/speech APIs, tenant/ownership guards and version snapshots are reused, with no schema migration. Raw PCM voice is saved before optional local ASR, remaining playable if transcription is unavailable. Ordinary typed queries/actions and C01 speech remain. [API/limits/acceptance contract](xiaok-assistant-phase-2.md#site-capture-and-delivery-contract).

Initial implementation evidence includes 42 focused checks, 5 real-service groups, 3 management-role checks, 2 fixed qwen voice pipelines, [native layout/login/revocation](../tmp/site-capture-20261107/native.json) and [upload/voice subchecks](../tmp/site-capture-20261107/native-r4/native.json). The earlier batch retains its original failures. Full lint reported 0 errors / 31 warnings. Webpack compiled and the complete build was blocked by inherited generated type syntax. General language/cross-turn correction remains Limited, with physical capture, noisy-dialect ASR and production rollout unverified.

The current construction chat follows V3 for the photo bubbles, receipt pills, capture actions and quick-work sheet, and the approved 05 paper card for manager review. A single received thumbnail is 144×98 and the local pending variant is 110×80. Time appears as “今天 HH:mm” or a dated time for older messages. Manager draft actions share one row with a 44px publish target and a 32px visible pill. Empty optional facts are available in the edit form, while the card groups supplied facts, scope and history. The same authenticated original and existing record mutations back preview, edit, retake and publication. [Approved states and calibration](../design-references/cloud-site-redesign-20261107/README.md#05-通用现场上报与回执已批准实施) · [generated asset mapping](./icon-sources/assistant/README.md).

**Unified FastGPT renovation conversation: Limited. The fixed native four-question journey now passes end-to-end in the isolated workflow; the candidate stays explicit opt-in, non-default and non-production.** Explicit `XIAOK_FASTGPT_WORKFLOWS_FILE.rag.mode=unified` and `rag.grounding=native-chat-quote` select the isolated v4.17.4 workflow: native classifyQuestion → original permission/query gate → native datasetSearchNode → original-source verification → native ToolCall as a bounded web reader (its stopTool hands already-read pages to the answer node) → native chatNode as the sole final answerer with the unmodified v4.17.4 strict quote template (`fastgpt/vendor/strict-quote.ts`). General/public questions keep the native empty-query branch with no enterprise material or embedding; enterprise/mixed questions retrieve before answering. qwen3.7-plus and Bailian text-embedding-v4 remain fixed. No answer-prompt clauses, keyword router, model switch, second Agent, facts store or answer rewrite were added. Unknown classifications fail closed. The bridge accepts the vendor's merged adjacent-Human turn shape in every phase; a web-needing turn may ground on the still-authorized earlier web reads of the same conversation when the reader produced no fresh page, and fails closed only with no authorized web basis at all.

C01 `packages/assistant/chat/chat`, `/api/miniprogram/assistant/conversations/*`, schemas, markup/styles/assets and the approved390×844 design remain unchanged. Only explicitly opted-in text consultation uses this candidate. Original services still own durable writes, authorized model-original history, citation catalogs, current tenant/owner/audience/version/hash checks, source-dependent reentry, idempotency and per-call accounting. FastGPT uses `NO_RECORD_HISTORIES`; native CITE IDs resolve only selected real sources, and web citations use per-turn short IDs (`w:<run>:<index>[:<part>]`) each verified at save against that run's authorized original read. The fixed four-document corpus becomes unavailable when any required publication loses access. Web tools retain the original search/read, SSRF/DNS/TLS and privacy boundaries; candidate snippets are not Evidence. A classifier call is separately metered and cannot become the stored answer.

The fixed two-by-four journey completed under this workflow in the real C01 conversation (`quote-bounded06`/`quote-bounded07`). Group one retained two reviewed originals, resent the web question through the normal input box after its recorded `invalid_model_citation` first draft (11033), and completed the mixed recall turn after its recorded `invalid_message` composition failure (11035). Group two ran all four questions fresh with real inputs, sends, actual citation taps, exact reentry, audience withdrawal hiding dependent answers while the general reply stayed readable, restoration, old-session revocation with refreshed-identity reentry, and an actual HTTP503 recovery; the proxy ledger shows zero secondary native-title calls. Both failures were real product defects fixed at the mechanism level, not wording patches: the bridge now accepts the vendor's merged adjacent-Human turn shape in every phase, and a web-needing turn reuses the still-authorized earlier web reads of the same conversation. 11025's original answer remains a failed historical draft. Review limitations recorded per first draft: length/markdown weight and slip-resistance generalization on general drafts, the matte correction sometimes restated weakly, the outside-source wall-sizing (墙固) suggestion, marketing-copy density and duplicate same-page web citations.

Independent `workflow-grounding/saved-permissions01` used those same two saved originals with zero model calls. Stable DevTools verified exact readback/reentry, an actual PPT19 citation tap, real audience withdrawal hiding the dependent answer while retaining the general reply, restoration, and an actual HTTP503/recovery. Cross-user/tenant denial and old-identity rejection/refreshed reentry also passed. This verifies access control over saved drafts, not the failed answer's semantics; the web/mixed journey completion above is separate evidence. The approved C01 source is `design-references/xiaok-chat-redesign-20261029-v1/tab-context-v1/README.md`; native captures retain its existing layout, including the original source-unavailable state. Physical-device behavior remains unverified.

Thirty-one focused checks and scoped types/lint pass; full lint has zero errors/31 existing warnings. Source/configuration bindings were stable across the classified batch and390-file saved-access audit. Unconditional retrieval11001 remains a failed source-relevance experiment.11022/11023 failed before any configured-model call because reference inputs were hidden and the setup saved a draft without publishing it. The fix uses `isPublish:true`, checks a changed publishedVersionId, and verifies published input values/types via version/detail. <!-- v1-02-report-composition -->
**Natural report composition and original-source readback: locally accepted for V1-02.** After the original local fact transaction, `construction.report.compose` is admitted through the existing `newRun` budget/ledger. It binds report/revision, recipient/binding, original conversation generation, dispatch epoch, project set, period/cutoff and fact hash; it does not read ordinary chat history or generated old reports. `report-body-worker.ts` reuses `runRenovationAgent`, `executePostgresText`, KnowledgeRetriever/Evidence, existing synthesis/citation catalogs and source resolvers. The internal retrieval scope supplies the original lease/project guard before and after I/O; candidate audience/publication predicates are unchanged. Final-only tool output uses the original shared projection, preserving the provider packet and billing evidence before source-sensitive completion. No additional Agent, facts store, permission resolver or citation pipeline is introduced. The report model receives project facts, authorized knowledge text, applicability and exact citation IDs. The read service presents knowledge titles and file locators from the revalidated citation. Report prose still passes the original citation, number and commitment checks. Eligible new scheduled report bodies use the existing 4096-token final allocation. Reservations, model, schema, review, and historical runs retain their original behavior. Truncated frames remain private to the owning run, and unknown outcomes remain ineligible for retry. For newly admitted supported scheduled bodies the configured model returns one named terminal function. The service rejects free text alongside the call, validates the same schema locally and then applies the original citation, quantity and commitment checks. Earlier bound runs retain their original channel. New bound bodies use server-defined project slots in the terminal tool. Each slot has exactly its authorized citation catalog and four required sections. The persisted body and read APIs continue to use the actual project identities. Eligible new finals can carry each project segment as a one-dimensional string field. The server reconstructs the original structure and runs the original schema, citation, numeric and commitment checks in full. Existing runs retain their bound terminal and grouping protocols. The flat terminal decoder treats a single empty citation string on an advice segment as an omitted optional citation. Nonempty IDs are preserved, and the reconstructed body still goes through the original citation catalog, evidence, quantity and commitment checks. Newly admitted runs can use a quote token from authorized project knowledge to insert the exact source span and bind its citation in the same segment. The flat terminal decoder also accepts a single citation-ID list in the third position and normalizes it losslessly. Raw provider frames and historical bindings are retained. The reconstructed body still uses the original project, source, quantity, commitment, authorization and revocation checks. Report body validation rejects single-character or punctuation-only fragments before storage. Newly bound scheduled weekly reports supply the original frozen stage entries from the inclusive period start to the exclusive cutoff, collapse consecutive identical stages and expose existing coverage limits. The original project, recipient, version, citation and revocation checks remain in force. Historical runs and explicit report follow-ups retain their original input projection.

Scheduled reports display authorized snapshot facts and verbatim workmanship excerpts. For newly admitted DashScope reports, the model selects one eligible pending item per project and can select a workmanship reference. The server turns the selected record into a request to verify. The item identifier is scoped to the original report, project, revision and item. Free-form claims, foreign items and stale identifiers fail validation. If the model omits the workmanship reference, the server uses the first eligible file-backed excerpt in the same authorized retrieval order. This displays the source as workmanship evidence and makes no determination about its applicability to the site. Existing authorization checks govern the source, body commit and subsequent reads. If no pending item is eligible, the report directs the recipient to the original records or the site team. Earlier runs retain their bound protocols. The V1-02 water-stage report scope passed local acceptance. Stable verified 10 daily and weekly report journeys across five destinations and all four roles, including original PDF downloads, viewer dispatches, saved read receipts and reentry. The two owner daily source controls were tapped. For generic detail, the harness verified the rendered control and invoked its existing native handler. Actual device, WeChat login and system viewer operation remain unverified.

**Default testing policy (user-confirmed 2026-10-17):** Codex runs Mini Program
tests through the verified Stable `2.02.2608080` installation at
`D:\微信开发工具\微信web开发者工具`, official `miniprogram-automator@0.13.0`,
base library `3.17.2`, and the `390x844` logical viewport. The replacement passed
13/13 basic automation checks, including page data, element queries, real taps
and inputs, navigation, and screenshots; this does not establish acceptance of
untested business flows. Future work uses this workflow and the window/route/
fresh-compilation rules in `AGENTS.md`, without renewed automation approval or
required manual tests/screenshots. Capture and review affected native routes
automatically; record physical-device/BLE and other uncovered checks as
unverified, without assigning user hand-tests. This policy supersedes earlier
automation cancellations and manual-QA requests below; their missing evidence
remains unverified. Product behavior and deployment boundaries are unchanged.

**Session recovery: Implemented.** Database failure during server-side identity
lookup no longer impersonates an invalid login. Status-aware routes return 503,
so the existing client error/retry paths keep the valid session. A delayed 401
from an earlier login/identity also cannot clear the current session; genuine
current-session invalid or revoked identities still fail authentication. Runtime source: the existing
lead-form error handling and shared `utils/api.js` request path. No WXML, Less,
assets, route entries, or permission boundaries change.

**Native regression fixes: Implemented.** Customer profile saves preserve JSON
`null` for optional community/style/city fields instead of persisting the text
`"null"` and exposing it in the existing lead-card style badge. A result link
without a task ID leaves loading and reuses the existing Design-tab fallback.
The staff-only inspiration page waits for signed identity hydration and checks
the existing `staff.design` route capability before reading; forbidden customer
or referrer deep links return to their existing role landing without clearing
the session. `GET /api/inspirations` now accepts the signed designer capability
within its enterprise through the shared Mini Program transaction scope;
customer/referrer access is 403 and Admin cookie writes remain unchanged.
Construction identities can read the existing public numeric enterprise-brand
route; lead/finance/survey/Admin routes remain denied. Sources are the existing
role-shell lead card, result fallback, role navigation, inspiration UI and
construction brand navigation. Those corrections preserve WXML/Less/artwork.
Construction chat additionally restores the already specified compact button
geometry from PPT22/38: back `42rpx`, composer icons `66rpx`, send `70rpx`.
Explicit Mini button sizing and targeted selectors prevent WeChat's default
184px button width from pushing branding under the capsule and send offscreen.
Artwork, source selection, APIs and publication/permission boundaries remain.

Formal-plan recovery also avoids persisting an empty initialization canvas while
its existing cloud graph is still loading. Exiting during that load therefore
cannot create a newer empty backup that hides the saved graph on reentry.
Real local geometry and an intentional clear after loading retain normal
persistence; graph validation, v4 serialization and API permissions are unchanged.

Add `generated/xiaok-chat-avatar.png`→`xiaok-chat-avatar.png` in the same runtime directory (256×256/49,659bytes), and `generated/xiaok-chat-room.png`→`xiaok-chat-room.png` (780×380/51,118bytes). All three sources live under `design-references/xiaok-assistant-phase1-ppt-v1/` and are optimized transparent PNGs. Avatar alpha bounds x27–236/y11–253 preserve the complete character inside a68rpx image. Six local Lucide1.8.0 PNGs and the existing standalone brand logo are retained; licensing is in `docs/icon-sources/assistant/README.md`. Voice/image upload, floor-plan understanding, automatic requirement summaries and human takeover remain outside phase one.

XiaoK operations monitoring and retention backends are **Implemented**; phase one remains **Limited**. `GET /api/assistant/operations` requires the tenant's active enterprise_admin and explicit `assistant-usage`, returning UTC-window answer metrics, current queue/unknown-cost backlog, configurable threshold alerts and honestly unknown heartbeat/provider health. `npm run assistant:retention -- --enterprise-id <id> --admin-id <id>` defaults to dry-run; explicit `--apply` requires database `assistant-retention` and configured retention_days. Batches of100 redact only expired archived/closed conversation content, excluding live/unknown/unreconciled work. IDs, idempotency, financial audits and knowledge sources remain; retained citations cannot restore redacted content. No new UI, default permission, production schedule or schema migration. The serial operations regression and separate full-restore drill both pass in isolation; current evidence and remaining production/A10/UI requirements are in [phase one, section9](./xiaok-assistant-phase-1.md#9-operations-retention-and-compatible-migrations).

XiaoK status: `Limited`. See the [roadmap](./xiaok-assistant-roadmap.md) and [phase-one implementation contract](./xiaok-assistant-phase-1.md) for 1A evidence. New customer entry-context, conversation create/list/detail/archive, message send/history, and run read/cancel/retry APIs live under `/api/miniprogram/assistant/*`; the CRON_SECRET-protected worker lives at `/api/internal/assistant/jobs/run`. Migrations 0058/0059 provide 13 new tables with forced RLS and composite tenant FKs. Valid WeChat customer identities may read their own participating conversations without a phone; staff/referrer/Admin/platform identities gain no default access. Service codes only resolve and audit; no lead, attribution, or commission side effect. Message/run/job/idempotency/quota commit atomically, with lease recovery, unknown-outcome retention, and cancellation fencing. Enterprise reset/purge includes the new data.

The 1B knowledge backend is **Implemented**; phase one remains **Limited**. `/api/assistant/knowledge/documents` and `[id]` provide list/create/detail/metadata operations; `[id]/versions` clones drafts; `[id]/versions/[versionId]` edits them; `index`/`publish` index and publish; `[id]/retire` withdraws; `/api/assistant/knowledge/jobs/[jobId]` reads job status. Writes require Idempotency-Key and the applicable expectedVersion/expectedEditVersion. Only active bound enterprise_admin users with explicit database menu_permissions entry assistant-knowledge may access these APIs; no platform exception. Index execution rechecks permissions before/after I/O. This batch adds no menu or default grants. Migration0060 adds five knowledge tables (documents/versions/chunks/profiles/attempt audit), forced RLS and composite FKs. Knowledge jobs fence leases/hash/edit_version; publication/withdrawal increment epoch and write outbox events. Reset/purge includes this data.

The 1B grounded-answer, structured-citation/invalidation, insufficient-evidence and owned-preview backend is **Implemented**. The same ConversationService/JobQueue invokes the existing text model after authorized retrieval. Every answer segment cites actual evidence and exact quotations; final commit rechecks permissions, epoch, hash/profile and validity. Empty/weak evidence or an explicit model insufficiency result produces a fixed insufficient_evidence notice. Retrieval outages, invalid references and malformed output never masquerade as valid answers. Migration0061 adds a forced-RLS citation table and message/run answer_kind; citations, final message and terminal state commit atomically. Reset/purge includes citations. Later reads hide withdrawn/restricted/expired/edited-draft sources and the entire dependent answer; still-authorized old publications are historical.

FAQ answer composition is **Implemented** with `extractive-selection-v3-faq`: question headings are matching context, not selectable answers; short answers (at most400 characters) retain their complete conditions. Selection prioritizes one complete direct answer and adds only necessary non-repeating information; identical selected text is deduplicated. Delivery removes only FAQ question/answer framing, while citation snapshots remain exact original spans and final validation allows no invented text or dropped answer qualifications. Existing saved messages are not rewritten. Development enterprise1454/customer645/lead1818 now answers the reported water/electric preparation question with one text block and one available citation; conversation109/run80 contains exactly one user message and one assistant message, unchanged on history reread. Evidence: `tmp/xiaok-vector/faq-answer-live.json`; regression coverage is in `answer-selection.test.ts` and `assistant-answers.test.ts`. Routes, APIs, permissions, Mini markup/styles/assets and approved PPT design are unchanged.

New `GET /api/miniprogram/assistant/citations/[citationId]`; Admin `/api/assistant/preview/conversations`, detail/archive/messages, `/runs/[runId]` plus cancel/retry, and `/citations/[citationId]` reuse conversation services. Only a bound enterprise_admin with assistant-knowledge may access their own preview; platform roles, other administrators and customers cannot. Creation fixes audience and up to10 ready draftVersionIds/hash/editVersion; sending cannot expand scope. Citations mark isDraft and previews consume the same enterprise quotas. Customer locators return participating-conversation routing IDs only before tenant authorization.

The 1B usage aggregation, statistics and unknown-cost reconciliation backend is **Implemented**. `GET /api/assistant/usage` returns UTC-day text/query-embedding usage, indexing calls, provider states, run purposes/errors, costs and current quota. The default is30 days, maximum90, with an exclusive end date; integers are decimal strings, unknowns remain null, and known subtotals are accompanied by unknown counts. `GET /api/assistant/usage/reconciliations` pages unresolved reservations; `GET/POST /api/assistant/usage/reconciliations/:reservationId` reads safe audit details/submits review. Active enterprise-bound enterprise_admin accounts require explicit database `assistant-usage`; submission additionally requires `assistant-usage-reconcile`. No default grants or menus are added. Submission requires Idempotency-Key, expectedVersion, tokens, costMicros, currency=CNY, evidenceReference and reason; observed tokens must match. Only terminal runs with no live lease and dispatch older than180 seconds can be reviewed. Migration0062 adds forced-RLS/composite-FK review audit, input/output tokens, confirmed quota and a fixed CNY budget currency. Idempotent settlement shares the settings lock; unresolved holds survive midnight. Review neither changes run state nor resends calls nor charges customers. Reset/purge includes reviews. Historical query usage is not fabricated; local embedding hardware cost is unpriced. Unverified tokens or costs remain reconciling.

The 1C backend fixes and isolated synthetic acceptance are **Implemented**; the overall phase remains **Limited**. Final selection is SiliconFlow’s free Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B with thinking disabled:50/50 answerable cases (baseline38/50),10/10 unsupported refusals, zero timeouts/invalid citations,5.194s P95, passing live three-turn and answers16/16, and complete-entry passed=true. R1 was prioritized but still timed out on revalidation; failures are retained and no paid backup is enabled. Models select authorized evidence identifiers; the server renders and strictly validates verbatim citations. General policy is distinguished from project commitments, and signing-query synonyms reuse existing indexes. Local assistant-only model/non-thinking environment settings are saved without changing the platform default or other AI features. Explicit402 is a known failure still subject to cost review; unknown usage/cost is never filled with zero. Foundation30/30, knowledge9/9 and usage8/8 pass; test data/active queues are cleaned to0. Evidence and limitations are in phase-one section2. That isolated quality run did not change business data or ComfyUI; current development enablement is recorded above, and business A10 acceptance remains pending.

Development primary migration and 微云 integration are recorded above. Production cutover, real business knowledge quality and native UI acceptance remain pending; phase one remains Limited.

## 2026-09-03 Scheme re-edit reference preview correction

When `scheme-studio` reopens a timeline batch through **重新编辑**, the draft
now preserves the signed preview URLs returned in `batch.referenceAssets` while
retaining the persisted reference ids and site-photo role classification. The
configuration sheet therefore renders carried-over reference/effect images
instead of blank placeholders. Studio routes, APIs, tenant scope, permissions,
and generation payloads are unchanged; focused composer tests cover the
restored URLs.

## 2026-09-03 Scheme finalization feedback correction

In `packages/ai-workflow/scheme-studio/scheme-studio`, finalization is now shown
once at the workflow level instead of once per completed round. Unpublished
workflows show the prerequisite hint, while published workflows expose the
scheme-level action/state. The existing action gives explicit feedback when
called without an active customer-visible publication or after finalization.
Finalization writes `leadLifecycleEvents` (`scheme_finalized` and
`scheme_unfinalized`) and withdrawing the last active publication clears the
finalized pointer. The finalization API, tenant scope, role boundary, and
generation workflow remain compatible; finalized means customer-primary and
does not freeze later generation.

## Runtime and shared context

- Native WeChat Mini Program with custom tab bar, bright-green design tokens,
  and iPhone 13 Pro `390x844` as the visual baseline.
- Sessions use `/api/auth/miniprogram` and bearer JWT. `GET /api/miniprogram/bootstrap`
  validates the signed context and returns the current role, valid role groups,
  enterprise/membership context, landing path, capability allowlist, and
  a server-owned badge summary (`status`/`message`/`counts` keyed by the
  current role Tab). Badge load failure keeps identity bootstrap and returns
  `unavailable` with `暂时无法读取` instead of a local zero. Phone authorization can
  create an ordinary customer account; `wechat_phone` prefers the WeChat dynamic
  `code`, and older clients that only return `encryptedData`/`iv` decrypt with a
  pre-tap `loginCode`/`session_key`. The phase-3 referral claim endpoint can
  also consume WeChat authorization codes (including that legacy ciphertext) and atomically link the account,
  attribution, and lead. Server-side phone lookup uses WeChat
  `getStableAccessToken`; archiving a lead still releases the attribution lock
  so a later scan can create a new lead. Tokens select a database-validated
  `customer`, `staff`, or `referrer` context and are invalidated by
  `contextVersion`. Professional staff, enterprise context, leads, floor plans,
  AI tasks, commissions, and promotion records resolve through shared APIs.
  On launch/resume the client refreshes the stored token against the current
  context. Mini Program staff workbench roles are `designer`, `measurer`,
  `salesperson` (渠道地推; lands on enterprise promotion records),
  `enterprise_admin`, and `platform_admin` (mapped from `admin` / `super_admin`).
  User-facing Mini Program copy displays `designer` as 家装设计顾问 and
  `measurer` as 家装现场顾问; API role keys stay unchanged.
  Platform channel `salesperson` accounts may have a null `enterpriseId` and
  still bootstrap with capabilities `promotion.records` /
  `promotion.commissions` / `account`. Platform admins land on `packages/platform/devices/devices` (moved out of the main-package `platform-device-workbench` component):
  BLE scan collects only the current scan session's live `LDMStudio 4D` MAC broadcasts
  without connecting; it can be cancelled from the same control, then checkbox /
  assign-all posts to `POST /api/miniprogram/devices` with optional SN (`serialNumber`
  on a single-device shared field or per scanned item). An already enrolled MAC is
  rejected as `该设备已录入` rather than reassigned. The registered-device list has
  a separate 查看范围 picker that defaults to 全部企业 (`GET /api/miniprogram/devices`
  with no `enterpriseId`) and can still filter one enterprise via `?enterpriseId=`;
  assignment 归属企业 stays independent of the list scope. Enterprise staff
  still must pass `POST /api/devices/verify-binding` (enterprise ownership only)
  before a single measurement connection. Refresh work is token-versioned, so a
  stale cold-start failure cannot clear a newer phone-login session. An invalid active context clears
  local session state and normally enters explicit identity recovery. When the active or launching route
  is the public `free-design-service`, employee/referrer `onboarding`, or enterprise-registration QR
  landing, the same stale context is cleared without replacing that destination: code resolution and
  phone authorization continue anonymously, so direct scans and WeChat share cards follow the same
  entry contract. A referrer
  context restores the promotion workbench instead of silently falling into the
  ordinary-customer shell.
- Primary actions use locally stored, license-documented icons. Native host
  capsule and safe areas remain outside the content lane.
- The source package explicitly excludes `tmp/` (including test logs), development folders, tmp preview
  scripts, and local design-token files through `project.config.json`
  `packOptions.ignore`; unused historical rasters were deleted from the source
  tree rather than left in ignore. The primary package keeps only its current
  runtime assets and leaves margin below the WeChat 2MB source-package limit.
  The scheme-studio-only Composer now lives at
  `packages/ai-workflow/components/ai-scheme-composer/`, with its model, local
  component registration and shared `sheetMotion` import updated together.
  The folio cover and the round-setup reference pair that only the AI workflow
  renders moved from `images/` into `packages/ai-workflow/assets/`, and the
  unreferenced `services/analytics.js` and `utils/filter.js` modules were
  deleted.

  455,883, assistant 1,828,840, surveying 1,901,944, guides 1,278,511, AI
  workflow 809,969, construction 247,233 and platform 79,039 bytes. The 14

  Phase 5 interaction and session separation is Implemented. `session/state-machine.js`

  Phase 6 reduces the kernel to a 97-line compatibility entry with no top-level domain functions; 76 modules / 400 dependency edges and 79 generated mirror pairs are audited. All 64 legacy and 69 facade exports remain available
  with explicit ownership. The 368 new tests include individual transition checks, both-client
  frozen command comparisons over every state, per-step preview/commit/snap comparisons in

  frozen Canvas-index comparisons. All 1,089 surveying/editor tests, 55 H5 tests, 39 Admin
  consumer tests and existing performance gates pass. Full-suite results are recorded in the
  [Phase 5 completion record](./surveying-module/legacy-kernel-phase5-interaction-state-machine.md).
  No UI, artwork, route, API, role, tenant permission, BLE protocol, measurement-audit queue or
  formal v4 data contract changes. Admin remains read-only. No new design source or DevTools
  automation is required. Phase 6 compatibility/runtime-source consolidation is complete; see [the Phase 6 completion record](./surveying-module/legacy-kernel-phase6-compatibility.md).
  Phase 7 governance is Implemented: pure geometry/read-model direction, acyclic dependencies,
  unique explicit facade/legacy exports, production isolation from the compatibility kernel,
  and the 79-file Mini/Admin manifest mirror are permanent executable gates. New graph writes
  must use the existing operations transaction, topology/Space synchronization and validator;
  host/BLE behavior remains in the editor. No route, API, role, permission, UI, BLE protocol,
  design source or version-4 contract changed. See the
  [Phase 7 closure](./surveying-module/legacy-kernel-phase7-governance.md).
  Test sources are excluded from every runtime package. These internal refactors
  have no visible UI or design-source change, need no WeChat DevTools automation,
  and change no route, API, role, permission, snap/closure rule, error copy, or
  version-4 data contract.
  The three surveying guide
  poses under
  `packages/surveying/assets/surveying-guide-k-*-v3.png` are indexed-palette
  transparent PNGs so that subpackage stays under the same 2MB source cap.
  Main-package artwork under `images/` is
  palette-optimized in place. The
  `packages/business` and `packages/enterprise` subpackages stay under the
  separate 2MB subpackage source-size cap by packaging only runtime artwork
  (palette-optimized PNGs) and loading Xiao K mascots from the main-package
  `images/airy-v1` copies instead of duplicating them under either package.

  `packages/platform` ordinary subpackage (not independent: it reuses the main-package
  custom TabBar, `utils/api.js`, and `utils/bluetooth.js`) and stay under the
  same 2MB subpackage source-size cap.
  Role-guide pages and their generated artwork live in a separate
  `packages/guides` subpackage; runtime illustrations are palette-optimized
  transparent PNGs so the subpackage stays under the independent 2MB source
  cap, and each generated PNG remains at or below 300KB.
- `Implemented`, `Limited`, and `Placeholder` describe executable runtime
  behavior, not labels or mock responses.

## Page inventory

### Navigation measurement view rotation

The absolute azimuth shown on the action (for example `231°`) is a reading and
an input to entry-door-relative direction mapping, never a direct canvas angle.
The relative bearing is hysteresis-snapped to `0°/90°/180°/270°`; only a `20°`
trigger delta changes the selected axis, and the canvas eases between axes over
`420ms` with an inverse visual sign so the plan counter-rotates against phone
movement; it cannot settle at a diagonal angle. The navigation label and live
bearing readout are both `28rpx` so the active state remains legible.
Native modal action taps are stopped at the cover-view boundary and Canvas touch
handlers ignore the visible/closing calibration state, so confirming a location
cannot select a wall direction underneath the dialog.
Navigation mode does not auto-lock a direction from its sensor callback; the
operator must tap a candidate arrow explicitly.

### Shared Mini Program statistic formatting

### Runtime update check

`App.onLaunch` initializes the official WeChat `wx.getUpdateManager` once and
tracks `checking`/`downloading`/`ready`/`latest`/`failed` states. `onUpdateReady`
shows a restart prompt and calls `applyUpdate` after confirmation. The Mine
account panel exposes **检查当前版本** using the cached lifecycle result;
WeChat does not expose a developer-triggered `check()` method, so the button
cannot force a fresh network check. `getUpdateManager` is guarded for older
clients and unsupported runtimes. No route, API, permission, or tenant data
contract changes.

All compact KPI, summary-card, badge, points, and quantity displays now share native-template `miniprogram/utils/stat-format.wxs`. `stat.count` keeps small values literal and shortens larger values (`1.5千`, `5万`, `100万`); `stat.money` keeps the unit with the value (`5万元`, `10万元`, `100万元`, then `亿元`/`万亿元`); `stat.percent` removes unnecessary decimal zeroes. The formatter is used by workbench/operations, schedules, referral and earnings summaries, commission summaries, Mine, service/archive count labels, lead delivery counts, and AI recipe/workflow/history/result statistics. It is deliberately not used for a commission ledger row, a lead conversion detail, an editable form value, or another reconciliation/detail amount: those remain exact. This is a presentational-only WXS change with no route, API, permission, or data-contract change; the approved page structures and source artwork are retained. The affected `390x844` native-capsule states require the user's manual runtime capture for final visual confirmation.

Appointments retain a manual service address plus an optional WeChat-map `gcj02` location (`locationName`, latitude, longitude). Booking keeps manual building/unit/room entry and adds native `wx.chooseLocation`; the assigned designer, measurer, or enterprise owner can update the same location through the existing versioned `POST /api/appointments/[id]/address` authorization and audit. That endpoint resolves Mini Program staff identity before Admin JWT so a measurer is authorized by `staff._id === appointment.measurerId`, not the WeChat user id. Creating or updating an appointment copies an empty lead `communityName` in the same transaction from the map `locationName` when present, otherwise the typed service address (trimmed to 160 characters; never overwrites). Appointment detail still offers staff an explicit sync for historical empty communities via `PUT /api/leads/[id]`. A confirmed appointment with coordinates opens native `wx.openLocation` for its authorized viewer; the measurer calendar uses it for its navigation shortcut. Historical/manual-only appointments remain valid but explain that a map point is not recorded. Referrers never receive exact addresses or coordinates.

### Identity switch card presentation

`packages/business/identity-switch/identity-switch` presents every active server
identity context in a two-column native card grid. Role names, including
`家装设计顾问` and `家装现场顾问`, are always fully readable; card selection remains
local until the existing native confirmation exchanges the signed context token.
The current context is explicitly marked. The role-tool artwork is independently
generated, transparent PNG content under
`packages/business/assets/identity-switch/role-cards/`; no approved composite
design is sliced into the runtime package. This changes neither the route nor
the `GET/POST /api/miniprogram/identity-contexts` permission contract.

### Lead-detail referral marker

`packages/enterprise/lead-detail/lead-detail` consumes the already returned, authorization-scoped
`GET /api/leads/[id]` `referrer.displayName`. When a referral-network attribution exists, its
native Hero displays **推荐人：姓名** flush to its lower-right edge, using the existing green
formal-survey tab treatment; no list scope, endpoint, role boundary, or navigation changes. The marker is absent
for leads without a referrer. Focused layout tests cover the data binding and shared visual
geometry; native `390x844` capsule-host verification remains pending the user's manual screenshot.

## List pagination

Unbounded Mini Program lists share `miniprogram/utils/list-pagination.js` (`page`/`limit`, default 20, max 50) with `createPaginationMetadata` on the matching GET. Scroll-view lists bind `scrolltolower`; page-scroll portal lists use `onReachBottom` (distance 120). Footer copy is `正在加载...` / `已经到底了` via `.sfp-list-footer`. Filter/search resets to page 1; summary chips use COUNT totals, not the current page length.

Covered: `enterprise-staff`, `enterprise-referrers` own/flat views, `referrer-progress`, `referrer-earnings`, `staff-earnings`, `enterprise-commissions` (`status`), `commission-records` (`status`), `promotion-records` (`search`), platform `enterprises` / `devices` (device rows only), lead-detail `assignable-staff`, and recipe-project `GET /api/miniprogram/ai/studio/leads`. Not paginated: the owner `enterprise-referrers?view=network` branch aggregate, `customer-projects` ranking index, workbench preview caps, identity-contexts, calendar/day appointments, BLE scan session, the devices enterprise picker, and overdue todos (`GET /api/workbench/todos`, still internally capped at 200).

### Password-login first-change reminder

`packages/business/login/login` and `packages/business/account-security/account-security` implement the current password-login contract. `/api/auth/miniprogram` returns `requiresPasswordChange` only for password-authenticated flagged staff and may still sign that flag into the token. The login page still runs normal bootstrap and role landing; it shows a one-time native `wx.showModal` reminder so the user can enter the workbench and TabBar immediately. Choosing **去修改** opens the existing account-security page after landing; **稍后** stays on the role landing. Cold start and token refresh do not trap the session or re-prompt. Mini Program APIs are not locked by this flag. `PUT /api/miniprogram/account/password` still clears `admin_users.must_change_password`; the existing page then clears local session and requires login with the new password. WeChat authorization and phone quick login do not trigger this reminder. Shared password matching reports `invalid_credentials` or, after filtering by password, explicit `ambiguous_identifier`. Existing sessions are not proactively revoked; the latest flag is acquired at token refresh or the next password login. Status: Implemented. Design source remains `design-references/miniprogram-airy-minimalist-v1/30-account-security.jpg`; the layout is unchanged and authenticated `390x844` runtime confirmation is pending.

## Onboarding server diagnostics (Implemented)

The existing onboarding page calls the instrumented server routes
`POST /api/miniprogram/codes/resolve`, `POST /api/auth/miniprogram`,
`POST /api/miniprogram/onboarding/referrer`, `POST /api/miniprogram/onboarding/staff`,
and `GET /api/miniprogram/bootstrap`. After deploying the updated Admin, every
invocation emits `[MiniProgramRequest]` start/result and exception diagnostics
with a per-request ID (`X-Request-Id` response header), stage, duration, HTTP
status and business code. The logger omits payloads, query strings, credentials,
phone numbers and raw error/SQL text; it retains diagnostic error codes and
source locations. A `404 / code_not_found` is an existing business rejection,
not proof that the route is missing. Nginx access logs remain the source for all
HTTP requests; see [live logs](./production-deployment.md#live-request-logs).
Request/response bodies, UI, design sources, client environment selection,
navigation, authentication, tenant boundaries and persistence are unchanged.
Tests verify the five actual entry points and diagnostic privacy/response
preservation; production scan replay awaits operator evidence. The request
diagnostic portion is server-only; the code-refresh behavior below also changes
the Mini Program and therefore requires a new client build. It does not change
the visual layout or design source, so no new visual screenshot QA is required.

## Promotion and staff activity code refresh (Implemented)

`promotion-service-code` and `staff-activity-code` reload their metadata and
WeChat image on every `onShow`, including returning from another page. A cache
nonce is sent to the image endpoint, and the shared loader validates PNG/JPEG
signatures, writes each response to a unique local path, removes superseded files,
and ignores late responses after hide/unload. The existing retry and profile
recovery states remain in use; share paths are cleared while a new response is
pending. No new button or visual asset is introduced.

The repository now verifies that an active promotion/activity row's stored hash
matches the token derived from its current secret, entity ID and version. If a
legacy row was created under a different secret, the read path rotates that row
atomically and returns a resolvable next version. Normal matching rows are reused,
so opening a page does not invalidate a working code. This repairs stale codes
without changing scan attribution, identity, permissions, routes, or persistence
shape. A freshly deployed Admin image and newly compiled Mini Program are
required; previously distributed QR image files cannot be changed remotely.
## Platform enterprise registration APIs

The enterprise-register form now exposes an editable contact-phone input. Users may type the number, but submission still requires WeChat phone authorization and an exact match; the CTA reads **授权手机号并提交**, auto-submits after successful authorization, and keeps the form open with an inline correction message on mismatch.

`POST /api/miniprogram/codes/resolve` recognizes platform `er_` open-account tokens (including bare 32-char scenes) as `{ kind: 'enterprise_registration', displayName: '家客来企业入驻', valid: true }`. `POST /api/miniprogram/enterprise-registration` requires a Bearer JWT whose authorized phone exactly matches `contactPerson.phone`, validates the active `er_` code, and creates a `pending_approval` / `self_service` enterprise through the same `createSelfServiceEnterpriseApplication` helper as Web `/api/auth/register-enterprise`. Platform review/ops for that application live on Admin `/enterprises` and Mini Program `packages/platform/enterprise-review*` (`POST /api/admin/enterprises/[id]/status` and `POST /api/miniprogram/platform/enterprises/[id]/status` both via shared `applyEnterpriseStatusChange`: FSM, owner provision, and `enterprise_join_result` notify). Platform admins can also present the current active `er_` composed open-account poster from `packages/platform/registration-code/registration-code` (`GET /api/miniprogram/platform/enterprise-registration-code/image?variant=poster`, view-without-rotate, long-press save); non-`active` enterprises are not usable as Mini Program staff/referrer workbench contexts. The scan landing is not a sticky home: WeChat recents/home/desktop reopens (`1001`/`1023`/`1089`/`1090`/`1103`/`1104`) with any signed identity leave to role landing; a fresh QR scan or chat-card share keeps any signed identity, including workbench, on the form so they can apply. Custom nav reuses the claim-page back chevron (`navigateBack`, otherwise role landing or Mine) so a stack-root scan cannot trap the user. The same recents-leave and back-chevron contract applies to `ej_` onboarding and `rp_`/`sa_` claim landings. Ready/success/error/recovery still expose **去登录** as a native button that `reLaunch`s password login after clearing the incidental phone-auth customer session (Mine visitor gateway if relaunch fails); `ACCOUNT_CONFLICT` after approval is an already-account exit; phone authorization that already resolves as a workbench identity stays on the already-account state and asks with `wx.showModal` whether to leave (session hydration is pinned so it cannot steal the page); confirm goes to role landing, cancel stays so the message can be read, with password-login fallback if landing fails. Enter-scene detection uses live WeChat enter options so a hot-start scan is not mistaken for a recents reopen. Login `mode=password` does not bounce back to the scan page. On the ready form, empty required fields (enterprise name, unified social credit code, contact name) keep a mint `--action-disabled-bg` CTA that stays tappable: the page lists `还需填写：…`, and a tap highlights those inputs with inline `请填写…` copy. The contact-phone field is editable for manual entry, but the ready CTA is explicitly **授权手机号并提交**: WeChat `getPhoneNumber` validates that entry and automatically posts the registration on success; a mismatch leaves the form with an inline correction message. WeChat `getPhoneNumber` is bound only after those three fields are filled, so an incomplete tap is not a silent no-op. Status: API and `packages/business/enterprise-register/enterprise-register` Implemented; focused contract tests cover scene/`er_` restore, resolve-before-phone, missing-field hints, form-gated one-tap authorize-and-submit, phone-match submit, the login/workbench exit, a fresh QR keeping a signed workbench identity on the form, recents reopen leaving signed scan landings, stack-root back, and post-approval phone-auth prompting before leave. Authenticated `390x844` native-capsule visual QA remains pending. After platform approval the contact phone logs in as `enterprise_admin` with initial password `123456`; approval also links that phone's existing Mini Program user to the new admin account. Approval SMS and in-app progress lookup remain out of scope.

## Formal surveying

Measured inner/outer alignment on adjacent-room closure is Implemented: a confirmed wall keeps its pre-close body side even when collinear with another closed room’s outer extension. Inner and outer alignment remain distinct; an outer-aligned return retains its one-thickness connector instead of flipping the measured top wall inward or losing the clear-length difference. This applies to explicit closure and automatic Face completion. Inferred unmeasured connectors retain their boundary-side solver. The 96 new regressions cover 100/200/400 mm thickness, rotations/reflections, inner/outer alignment, direct/committed closure, unchanged original rooms and readings, and formal save/reload; all 96 fail against the old closure implementation. Behavior source: user-approved right → down → left sequence and `design-references/surveying/closure-face-alignment-20260907/{01-inner,02-outer,03-defect}.jpg` (Fig.3 is defect evidence). Existing UI/Canvas styling, routes, APIs, permissions, audit fields and v4 envelope are unchanged; saved rooms are not migrated. Native `390x844`/tall-device visual QA remains pending the user’s manual screenshots.

Verification for this correction: 96 new regressions pass (0/96 with the old closure code), 39 Admin consumer tests and 55 H5 tests pass, and the 83-file generated mirror check passes. The full Mini Program suite passes 1,815/1,856; all 41 failing test names match the 1,719/1,760 pre-fix control run, including pre-existing `wall.use` frozen-baseline differences. Logs are in `tmp/closure-face-alignment/`. Generated Admin mirrors also include the pre-existing validator/commit-preview source edits, whose behavior this correction preserves.

Outer-aligned adjacent-room closure is corrected (Implemented). Releasing an
unchanged wall preview, continuing it, or confirming it directly preserves its
visible endpoint; it does not rerun rectangle snapping and shift the cursor to
an inner closure guide. Beyond a closed-room corner, the closer physical outer
axis wins over the neighbouring inner rectangle axis. When that endpoint lies
on the returning boundary's outer extension, the suggested and confirmed path
follows that outer axis, then bridges to the topology corner; it does not extend
the last measured wall or offset the return wall from the shared wall's body.
Explicit manual/BLE length corrections retain their existing snap behavior and
do not switch an inner preview to an outer axis. Reference: the user's four
images in `design-references/surveying/closure-outer-alignment-20260904/`
(`01-drag.jpg` and `03-expected-close.jpg` define the expected behavior).
The production wall-union geometry in `tmp/survey-outer-alignment/closed-geometry.png`
matches the continuous junction in image 3. Twenty-five geometry regressions
cover 100/200/400 mm walls, rotations, mirrors, direct/committed closure, unchanged
readings and the original room; the editor regression covers release, explicit
close, and undo/redo. Updated `390x844` and tall-device runtime captures remain
pending the user's manual screenshots. Existing layout, artwork, routes, APIs,
permissions, measurement auditing and version-4 storage are unchanged; saved
rooms are not retroactively reshaped.
Verification: the complete Phase 7 gate passes (1,145 survey/editor tests,
39 Admin consumer tests, 55 H5 tests plus build, 79-file mirror/architecture
checks and performance thresholds). The full Mini Program run passes
1,634/1,648 tests; the remaining 14 failures exactly match the recorded
`surveying-module/legacy-kernel-phase0-baseline.md` list. `git diff --check`
and bilingual route/evidence checks pass.

Manual clockwise/counterclockwise rotation in `packages/surveying/editor/surveying-editor`
recenters the rotated bounds of the current survey nodes and active preview point.
An empty draft retains screen-centre world-point compensation. This is view-only:
graph geometry, routes, APIs, permissions, and persisted viewport fields are unchanged.
Navigation-measurement axis changes use a different viewport policy from those
manual controls: each `420ms` rotation fits the closed plan, active wall chain,
preview/closure points, and current cursor into one centred spatial context in
the usable canvas. The current scale is retained when the complete context fits;
otherwise the view only zooms out far enough to keep it visible. Navigation
disable still returns to the existing whole-content-centred, unrotated view.
Markup, styling, BLE semantics, graph persistence, routes, APIs, and permissions
are unchanged.
While navigation measurement is active, tapping empty canvas after selecting a
direction arrow clears only that transient arrow lock. It restores all manual
candidate arrows without switching `bleDirectionMode` to `manual` or stopping
the shared heading subscription, so the live azimuth and canvas rotation keep
following the phone. Standalone automatic direction picking retains its prior
clear-to-manual behavior to prevent immediate sensor relock.
The paired left-side actions use separate generated transparent PNGs at
`packages/surveying/assets/icons/angle/{rotate-counterclockwise-v2,rotate-clockwise-v2}.png`
with a `24rpx` vertical gap supplied by the first button's explicit margin rather
than flex `gap`, so their arrowheads clearly identify the two directions.

Cloud-save contract: autosave, manual save, and completed submission share one serialized queue; only one request is in flight, and a queued `completed` request upgrades and takes priority over queued `draft` work. New floor-plan creates carry a persisted `Idempotency-Key`; the server-side unique `floor_plans.create_idempotency_key` makes a lost-response retry return the original plan instead of creating a duplicate. Once `floorPlanId` exists the client sends exactly one PUT; every PUT failure is propagated without clearing the ID or falling back to POST. The PUT endpoint authorizes the plan owner or the currently assigned linked-lead collaborator in the same enterprise, so reassigned designers/measurers can complete an existing plan saved by the other field role. POST is used only when no ID exists.

Floor-plan writes keep the formal-v4 400 envelope gate. Both draft and completed plans run `full` validation; completed plans also require no pending measured closure and at least one closed Space before any database write or preview generation. Invalid data returns 422 with the first error code/message plus `validation.mode/errors/stats`, and the server never repairs the submitted graph. Full validation rejects proper crossings, unsplit T endpoints, coincident endpoints owned by different node IDs, and positive-length collinear overlaps; it also requires mode-valid saved `lengthMm` / `angleDeg`, non-negative integer measurement inset/extension fields that leave positive effective measured length, and a complete integer `rawMeasuredLengthMm` / `closureAdjustmentMm` pair whose sum equals the saved length. Synthesized zero-reading `closure-merge` / `closure-bridge` connectors without a raw reading remain valid. Geometry uses integer-millimetre centerlines and the existing epsilon, never the 350 mm snap tolerance. Manual and BLE remeasurement use the same full immutable transaction. Isolated closed orthogonal cycles balance only along the remeasured wall's axis, so a later perpendicular remeasurement does not undo the first one; both open and closed remeasurement reject `OPENING_REMEASURE_CONFLICT` before moving a wall that could no longer contain its door/window. Invalid edits roll back graph, rooms, openings, history, and draft rather than auto-splitting walls or moving openings.

For an isolated orthogonal chain with no openings, shared nodes, or branches, a return-to-start residual is balanced over same-axis walls by measured-length weight. The ordinary 350 mm snap tolerance remains unchanged. A long multi-corner chain may use additional accumulated residual only when every wall remains inside a correction budget equal to 2% of coordinate length, clamped to 25–150 mm, and total residual does not exceed 1,000 mm. A short loop that would overspend one wall's budget is rejected instead of being distorted or closed with a micro bridge. Preview and confirmation use the same plan, keep segment directions and minimum lengths, include the tolerated 1 mm perpendicular straight-wall drift in the projected residual, and project the adjusted chain before offering closure; any new non-adjacent intersection, overlap, or contact with another wall rejects the plan. Confirmation stores each raw reading and its derived closure correction separately, and later endpoint inset, collinear merge, and wall split operations preserve that traceability.

Measurement audits use a local write-ahead queue: each accepted manual/BLE edit is persisted before upload, initially under a stable draft scope, then atomically moved and bound when `floorPlanId` becomes available. Loading or saving retries pending records; exceeding 500 records warns without truncation, and records bound to another plan are never sent. Embedded-component keyboard edits are batched into one final manual audit, and a pending-wall BLE commit records the real created `wallId`. The 17-byte ATD parser requires the vendor header/tail/CRC, preserves raw frame/channel/time metadata, treats distance as big-endian unsigned and angles as big-endian signed, deduplicates only identical cross-channel frames within 350 ms, and guards late app responses from becoming hardware-key readings. Closing the editor restores the prior complete BLE callback set. Uploads send canonical top-level `auditId` and retain `metadata.auditId` for compatibility; formal surveying requires a non-empty value of at most 200 characters. PostgreSQL's nullable `measurements.audit_id` partial unique index on `(floor_plan_id, audit_id)` is the final idempotency guard: a first create returns 201 / `deduplicated: false`; a repeat returns the same record with 200 / `deduplicated: true`. Existing null audit rows remain unchanged.

This stability hardening keeps every previously correct survey path and UI unchanged: snap/closure tolerances, multi-room shared walls, face extraction, wall solids, Canvas, WXML/Less, and the operator flow remain the baseline. In addition to the bounded near-close adjustment and remeasurement corrections above, the targeted corrections cover four internal-divider defects: an L-shaped divider keeps reused exterior walls on the source-room face instead of forcing `offset`; a divider continued after a short confirmed first segment stops at the first opposite-boundary hit instead of crossing the room wall; splitting a wall shared by two closed rooms freezes its already-rendered body side before cloning replacement segments, so a leftward partition cannot flip one segment by a wall thickness merely because post-sync Space order differs; and a divider that would split through an existing door/window is rejected before wall mutation instead of remapping that opening onto one replacement segment. The shared-wall correction changes only affected `bodyNormalSide` values. The opening correction reuses the existing non-layout error-toast path; centerlines, measurement faces, openings, Spaces, history, route/API/permission boundaries, WXML/Less, and persistence remain unchanged when it rejects a closure. Outward adjacent-room behavior remains unchanged.

The 4,096-case deterministic formal-closure catalog now exercises orthogonal and diagonal outlines, effective release tolerance, same-wall adjacent rooms, sequential four-room cross partitions, nearest-boundary partitions inside concave rooms, every source/divider wall-thickness pair, short manual/BLE divider continuations, safe door/window remapping immediately beside split points, atomic door/window-conflict rejection, formal save/restore, and immutable self-crossing rejection across rotations, mirrored winding, measurement sides, face snaps, and direct/committed closure actions. This adds no WXML/Less, route, API, permission, or persistence change.

Opening-split contract: before any host wall is split, every interior cut is checked against the opening's physical span plus the junction clearance of one current/incident divider-wall thickness. A touching or overlapping cut throws `OPENING_SPLIT_CONFLICT` with `分隔线压到门窗，请先调整门窗位置`; the immutable transaction returns no partial wall/node/Space/opening/history mutation, and direct release, the “合” action, manual length entry, and BLE length entry surface that message through their existing Toast. Safe placements beyond the clearance still remap to one replacement host segment without changing the opening's world position. Cross-segment openings remain unsupported; the operator must move or remove the opening before retrying the divider.

The surveying editor synchronizes visible BLE state from the active session on `onShow`; its connect entry does not start a second discovery while that session exists. App-level silent reconnect notifications also call the editor's `updateBleConnected` hook.

The only measurement editor is
`packages/surveying/editor/surveying-editor`, entered with `leadId`
and/or `floorPlanId`. A lead-only entry without `floorPlanId` resolves that
lead's primary cloud plan before creating a blank canvas. The authoritative contract is
[`surveying-module/formal-surveying.md`](./surveying-module/formal-surveying.md).
Its `190rpx` high compact top action row keeps the `132×88rpx` Navigation
Measurement control fixed at the left without native `cover-view` clipping; its
`28rpx` label uses a `32rpx` line box so native glyph ascenders are not clipped;
Guide, Save, CAD, and Finish flex evenly through the remaining width with `12rpx`
sibling spacing and no wrapping, so every action remains fully visible at
`390x844` and narrower phone widths.
In the relocation dialog, **关闭导航测量** is a full-width `100% × 72rpx`
secondary action with a stable native hit area and pressed state; this prevents
`cover-view` from collapsing it into an unstyled text line.
`FloorPlan.layoutData` contains only version-4 `surveyGraph` data. Completed
`POST/PUT /api/floorplans` of a formal v4 plan persist a `surveyCanvasRenderer`
PNG snapshot on `floor_plans.preview_asset_id` and never into `layoutData`. Wall graph,
Canvas renderer, dimensions, BLE readings, audit queue, undo/redo, and formal-survey
entry defaults follow the same contract: guide mode is off by default (an explicit
top-bar preference is retained), while BLE direction input mode is on by default for
every editor entry. No route, API, permission, or persisted floor-plan data changes.
A straight-wall drag that extends the current collinear terminal wall keeps a
visible `mergeClosing` **合** action after release when the committed endpoint
still matches the preview; coalescing the two measured lengths no longer clears
that valid closure candidate.
The native dock label follows the same BLE state: it reads `测距 · 已连接` when
connected and `测距 · 未连接` when disconnected.
The
right-rail confirmed canvas-clear/restart action, and save failure behavior
must follow that contract. The dock 测距 action writes a BLE reading onto a
pending wall preview or a selected wall; the meter's hardware measure key
sends the same 17-byte ATD frame and uses that same write path without a second
dock tap. Tapping 测距 or receiving a hardware ATD before a wall is dragged
toasts「请先拉出一条墙」instead of asking to open numeric edit. The top-bar back
control is an 88rpx cover-view painted after the centered title overlay so the
title layer cannot steal the tap. Successful top-bar Save (`onSaveDraft`) persists the
draft then navigates back; cloud failure stays on the editor. Page `onHide`/`onUnload`
immediately flush the local draft and silent-save the v4 graph to the cloud; a newer
local draft wins over a stale cloud copy on reopen. Closed `spaces` written by `confirmClosure`,
`deleteWall`, and closed-wall splits come from half-edge faces; the same
transaction rejects the edit if saved spaces and extracted faces diverge.
`deleteWall` (and remasure complete / cancel-pending) clears remasure
`session.fixedNodeId` so a deleted free tip cannot leave a stale session node
reference. Door/window width and position are clamped to the clear host interval excluding incident wall bodies (minimum 100 mm), not a 60% wall-ratio cap. Tap hit order is opening → wall → closed-space interior; selecting a
closed room sets `selectedSpaceId`, paints blue fill/stroke with internal clear
dims, and switches the right rail to rename (`renameClosedSpace`) / delete
(`deleteClosedSpace`, exclusive walls only; shared walls kept).
The persistent top-bar CAD action is disabled until the cloud plan is
`completed`; it downloads through `GET /api/miniprogram/floorplans/[id]/export/dxf`,
which reuses Mini Program floor-plan access control and the same formal-v4 /
closed-space validation as the Admin endpoint. The file uses Chinese CAD layers
and millimetre DIMSTYLE. Walls are unioned inner/outer `LINE` faces with
opening jambs, not per-wall thickness rectangles. Hinged doors insert a `DOOR`
block on the opening face with an open 90° thick leaf, gray dashed arc, and 50mm jambs;
sliding doors stay double rails and windows use four inset in-opening lines away from the wall faces.
Dimensions are rotated linear entities from the shared closed-space plan
(`标注线-内墙` inner segments including wall-thickness ticks, `标注线` overall
lengths, `DIMTAD` 2 / `DIMGAP` 10; recessed L-notch spans stay local). Closed rooms write four-line
MTEXT (`\P` breaks) from the inner-face polygon. A cyan full-height right
title panel and yellow north arrow wrap the side-by-side floors. Company sits
at the top of that panel. Company and designer come from the linked customer
lead; customer phone and address are omitted. The app saves the DXF to its
file domain and opens the system document handler; an unavailable DXF handler
shows a transfer-to-CAD-device prompt. The file is generated on demand only.
Graph nodes stay on the centerline; working faces and one-sided bodies are
read models. Deleting a wall shared by two closed rooms punches
through that interface and merges them into one closed room, including when the
shared run has been split into collinear segments. A merged inner dimension
plan must remain defined after collinear inner corners collapse. A punch-through
L corner keeps rectangular wall solids and must not convex-miter into the room.
Node joins use local convex/concave predicates (outer miter, overlapping inner
rectangles, outer-only opposite-thickness steps); Admin
`surveyWallSolidPlan.js` uses the same generator. Inner-face punch-through keeps each remaining wall's original body side: the
inner L extends into the merged room, opposite-thickness collinear walls stay a
stepped facade and fill the outer step corner so inner faces stay aligned, and the two remaining
walls keep overlapping rectangular solids instead of a convex-mitered trapezoid.
Partitioning either side of a two-room shared wall freezes the wall's current
physical side before splitting it. All replacement segments with the same
`topologySourceWallId` inherit one `bodyNormalSide`; post-sync Space ordering
cannot flip only one segment. Left/right partitions remain mirror-equivalent,
and the untouched room keeps the same render boundary, net area, and clear dims.
Closed exterior-wall T branches retain one topology node and physical wall.
An inferred orthogonal close absorbs a collinear continuation into the last
measured wall rather than leaving a butt joint. Two new walls started from a
closed-room corner close against the existing boundary when the second wall
lands on an adjacent wall and completes a face with the start edge; axis
alignment to a distant corner without hitting an existing wall still does not
infer extra closing walls. Loading a saved draft also
folds remaining collinear degree-2 splices into one wall. Deleting a wall that
opens a single closed room restores the remaining loop as the active chain and
offers the missing-edge close when the dangling ends still determine it.
Successful direct, inferred-merge, shared-boundary, and partition closure clears
the prior `lastWallSnap*` cursor-drop memory before orphan-node cleanup, so
absorbing a collinear joint cannot roll back a valid close with
`MISSING_SESSION_NODE`.
When adjacent collinear segments select different physical faces at a shared-wall/exterior-wall transition, the room fill and net dimension read models insert an explicit wall-thickness orthogonal step instead of connecting the two offset face endpoints with a diagonal. Graph, measurement audit, route, API, permission, WXML/Less, and persistence contracts are unchanged.
Closed walls are filled and stroked once from their geometric union outline; room `wallFaceOverrides` boundaries are no longer superimposed over fused walls, eliminating extraneous seams at T-junctions and thickness steps. Renderer revision is updated to `wall-union-outline-v20` to refresh stale Admin preview snapshots.
When dragging or measuring a new wall towards an existing closed room, ray intersection (`findRayWallIntersection`) clamps preview and committed endpoints to the first contacted boundary, preventing new walls from penetrating closed room interiors; internal divider drags (`isPotentialPartitionDrag`) remain governed by the dedicated partition closure solver. When the closing wall of an adjacent room overshoots the contact boundary, the endpoint is clamped to the ray hit and synthesizes a shared-wall closure candidate, entering `closing` / `shared-wall` and auto-confirming closure without creating an unsplit T-junction node or throwing `UNSPLIT_WALL_T_JUNCTION`. No route, API, permission, or persistence contract changes.
Resetting the cursor onto either dangling vertex resumes that same open chain.
Selecting a wall is an object-edit overlay: an already placed cursor keeps the
same `anchorNodeId` and remains rendered at the identical plan coordinate while
the wall toolbar is open; selection does not move, dock, or recreate it.
Confirming a closed room automatically enters the same reset-cursor / wall-drop
state as tapping 重置光标, so the operator can drag the dock cursor to the next
start without a second tap. Restoring a saved closed room likewise normalizes a
residual `spaceClosed` session to wall-drop, so reopening immediately shows
光标拖动到墙体 instead of requiring a reset tap. The formal canvas cursor and both dock states
(重置光标 / 光标拖动到墙体) use the same Fig.1 green reticle glyph
(`drawCursorGlyph` + `icons/cursor-reticle.png`). Dock wall-drop drags aim that
reticle 24×40 CSS px upper-left of the finger and clamp the aim point to the
canvas; snap, the corner magnifier, and release all use the aim point, not the
finger pad. Dock input uses a 16 ms leading/trailing latest-point queue instead
of dropping intermediate touchmove updates. One placement index per stable
formal scene reuses visible vertices, inner/outer wall segments, and closed-room
alignment axes. Free-follow frames skip the full snap search and dirty-clear
only the reticle; retained wall/alignment locks slide continuously on the same
target while vertex locks stay fixed, using the existing 16 px acquire / 26 px
release hysteresis. Magnifier crops remain low cadence, and retained snap frames
no longer full-clear the overlay. The `132px` corner magnifier is now a square,
image-only crop with its dashed guides and centre crosshair; it no longer paints
snap-status or X/Y-coordinate captions. The live `L… / ∠…` metric pill shares
that row but shifts toward the canvas centre so neither overlay covers the other.
Canvas wall-endpoint drags keep a sticky grab delta from
touchstart and a south-east-biased hit (`surveyCursorAim`); they must not apply
the dock offset, so the first preview frame cannot invent a wall. The drag magnifier overlays a small green
crosshair at its centre and does not magnify that glyph. During that wall-drop wait (`wallSnapPending`), the
canvas still pans and pinch-zooms. Its lightweight gesture frame keeps the current green reticle visible at the same transformed plan coordinate with a stable screen-space glyph size, providing a continuous zoom reference. The projector can apply a page-level view-only `rotationRad` that is never persisted. The top action row keeps a visible `132×88rpx` **Navigation Measurement** action in an independent far-left slot (technical component: `survey-canvas-compass`), while Guide / Save / CAD / Finish remain a fixed-`20rpx`-gap group aligned to the far right instead of being distributed across the row. Its native `24rpx` **导航测量** label uses the independently generated transparent `packages/surveying/assets/icons/navigation-measure.png` (128×128, 7,858 bytes), and the UI no longer renders `N`. Tapping it opens entry-door calibration. The operator stands inside the room, keeps the phone level and still with its top edge facing the entry door, and must connect the BLE distance meter before confirmation. Nine circular samples with no more than `6°` spread establish the entry-door's real compass azimuth; the active action displays the live absolute azimuth (for example `231°`) rather than rewriting it as `0°`. `surveyDeviceOrientation.sharedHeadingSensorHub` prefers Compass and falls back to DeviceMotion, while `sharedDeviceMotionHub` validates beta/gamma level when available. Heading subscriptions pause on `onHide`/`onUnload` and resume on `onShow` for an active logical mode. Straight-mode BLE quick input keeps blue dashed cardinal guides visible from the first wall and draws three or four compact, single-layer, translucent-green candidate pointers, excluding the active chain's backtrack. Once a bearing is selected, all other candidates disappear and only a small blue arrow remains on that direction's guide. When a pointer's `28px` touch radius overlaps an opening or wall, the object hit wins so the operator can still select the wall and use **复尺**; a short tap beside the reticle follows the same object-selection path, while a moved gesture still starts wall dragging. Tapping otherwise empty canvas space clears the transient bearing and restores the candidate pointers without moving the cursor or changing walls. The wall is still committed only after a valid BLE distance. Automatic pick waits for privacy authorization, converts the difference between the live absolute azimuth and the captured entry-door azimuth into the room-relative cardinal bearing, applies circular median plus activation/switch hysteresis, and rearms after each wall. Cardinal view changes rotate along the shortest arc over `420ms` with ease-out deceleration; tapping the active action reopens relocation or permits disabling navigation. `bleLockedBearingDeg`, entry-door calibration, and view rotation remain editor-session state and are stripped from local/cloud graph serialization. Dimension labels use world wall angle plus view rotation (`resolveScreenEffectiveAngle`); room cards stay screen-axis aligned; the grid rotates with the plan. Graph geometry, ortho snap, BLE protocol semantics, route/API/permission boundaries, and persisted viewport data are unchanged; a wall tap only selects the wall for opening
placement, while the cursor is placed only by dragging the dock control onto the
canvas. A short tap on a closed-room fill selects that space during the same
wait (`selectSpace`); the wall/vertex toast appears only when neither snap nor
fill hits. In guide mode that reset-cursor state immediately
shows the Xiao K place-next-start tip even when closed-room dimension labels
fill the canvas; the dock-guide layout softens or force-places rather than
hiding the tip, and `closing` / `mergeClosing` keep the BLE direction
candidates visible alongside the `合` close action.
Inner/outer start selects the near/far point on the source boundary and the
corresponding first-wall inset; it does not choose opposite local faces for the
new branch. Every branch segment uses the graph-side working face and inherits
the physical-body side fixed by the first segment. An explicit first-wall
measurement-position toggle on a shared-boundary chain moves the red measuring
edge to the opposite face while keeping that inferred occupancy; later drags
must not overwrite that choice. Turn direction and the
source-space centroid cannot re-evaluate that side. Orthogonal touch input stays
on the internal graph, while the preview outline, orange/red path,
live-dimension endpoints, and green cursor remain coincident. Straight-mode
vertex, closure, or outer-face snaps may change at most one axis and must not
copy an off-axis vertex or wall-thickness offset onto the orange preview. The
physical outer face remains the contact/closure target while confirmation uses
a short orthogonal bridge for any remaining off-axis gap; the wall-drag lens reports the actual
snap type and shows a small green crosshair rather than the canvas Fig.1 reticle,
following the sticky grab aim point rather than a dock-style finger offset. Adjacent red edges
meet with equal endpoints, so beginning a second segment cannot shift the cursor
or red line by one wall thickness. Measurement inset/extension fields record
real boundary or closure adjustments only; an ordinary outer-start T turn does
not synthesize a wall-thickness adjustment. Preview, manual/BLE confirmation,
Canvas, and dimension consumers consistently calculate `topology length - start
inset + start extension - end inset`. Closed-room Canvas dimension lanes omit per-room clear spans (`room-clear`)
unless a closed space is selected (`session.selectedSpaceId`); then that room
gets a blue selection fill/stroke and internal clear dimensions, and the right
rail shows rename (`renameClosedSpace`) plus delete (`deleteClosedSpace`,
exclusive walls only). Selected-state `room-clear` merges collinear end-to-end
`innerSegments` into one continuous clear label per side so a neighboring
T-junction that splits one physical edge into multiple graph walls does not
fragment those dims; `building-overall` and `space.wallIds` stay unchanged.
Unselected rooms keep building-overall bands plus door
positioning / wall-thickness ticks, keep recessed L-notch spans on the local
face, and sit outside every unclosed wall on the canvas plus a stationary length
preview; an in-flight `wallPreview` drag does not move those lanes. Closed wall
solids always retain topology endpoints so an adjacent-room T does not leave a
wall-thickness notch on the outer face. A door or window mask cuts only the host
wall and paints overlapping adjacent bodies back, so a door against a T or L
junction cannot punch a hole through the neighbouring closed solid. This derived Canvas projection does not
change graph centreline/closure topology. From the second branch wall onward,
turns may
join the rendered wall solids but cannot rewrite preceding measurement insets
